				<div id="bodyContent" class="vector-body" aria-labelledby="firstHeading" data-mw-ve-target-container>
					<div class="vector-body-before-content">
							<div class="mw-indicators">
		</div>

						<div id="siteSub" class="noprint">From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia</div>
					</div>
					<div id="contentSub"><div id="mw-content-subtitle"></div></div>
					
					
					<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content"><div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-mw-parsoid-version="0.24.0.0-alpha21" data-mw-html-version="2.8.0"><section data-mw-section-id="0" id="mwAQ"><div class="shortdescription nomobile noexcerpt noprint searchaux" style="display:none" about="#mwt1" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Short description","href":"./Template:Short_description"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Data structure which is efficient to both store in memory and query"}},"i":0}}]}'>Data structure which is efficient to both store in memory and query</div><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt1"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_short_description"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Short_description_is_different_from_Wikidata"/></span>
<p id="mwAw">In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Computer_science" title="Computer science" id="mwBA">computer science</a>, a <b id="mwBQ">succinct data structure</b> is a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Data_structure" title="Data structure" id="mwBg">data structure</a> which uses an amount of space that is "close" to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Information-theoretic" title="Information-theoretic" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBw">information-theoretic</a> lower bound, but (unlike other compressed representations) still allows for efficient query operations.  The concept was originally introduced by Jacobson<sup about="#mwt2" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-jacobson1988succinct_1-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"jacobson1988succinct"}}'><a href="#cite_note-jacobson1988succinct-1" id="mwCA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCg">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCw">]</span></span></a></sup> to encode <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bit_vector" title="Bit vector" class="mw-redirect" id="mwDA">bit vectors</a>, (unlabeled) <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tree_(data_structure)" title="Tree (data structure)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwDQ">trees</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Planar_graph" title="Planar graph" id="mwDg">planar graphs</a>. Unlike general <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lossless_data_compression" title="Lossless data compression" class="mw-redirect" id="mwDw">lossless data compression</a> algorithms, succinct data structures retain the ability to use them in-place, without decompressing them first. A related notion is that of a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Compressed_data_structure" title="Compressed data structure" id="mwEA">compressed data structure</a>, insofar as the size of the stored or encoded data similarly depends upon the specific content of the data itself.</p>

<p id="mwEQ">Suppose that <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt3" id="mwEg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"Z"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle Z}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>Z</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle Z}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/1cc6b75e09a8aa3f04d8584b11db534f88fb56bd" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.68ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle Z}"/></span> is the information-theoretical optimal number of bits needed to store some data. A representation of this data is called:</p>

<ul id="mwEw"><li id="mwFA"><i id="mwFQ"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Implicit_data_structure" title="Implicit data structure" id="mwFg">implicit</a></i> if it takes <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt4" id="mwFw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"Z + O(1)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle Z+O(1)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>Z</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle Z+O(1)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/2d618585770dc503ad46ec151d408ee0970f2b1f" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:9.266ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle Z+O(1)}"/></span> bits of space,</li>
<li id="mwGA"><i id="mwGQ">succinct</i> if it takes <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt5" id="mwGg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"Z + o(Z)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle Z+o(Z)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>Z</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>o</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>Z</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle Z+o(Z)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/073c03d0d8761f39809ce5c7edad5e01b99b2d39" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:9.138ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle Z+o(Z)}"/></span> bits of space, and</li>
<li id="mwGw"><i id="mwHA">compact</i> if it takes <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt6" id="mwHQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(Z)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(Z)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>Z</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(Z)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/4cbf219f550497e82c5fc81700bd54b5d5420188" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:5.263ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(Z)}"/></span> bits of space.</li></ul>

<p id="mwHg">For example, a data structure that uses <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt7" id="mwHw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"2Z"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 2Z}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mn>2</mn>
        <mi>Z</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle 2Z}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/5b5d7e0cd50d0be9ed3acb6d3e9eed2e4284f8b5" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:2.843ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 2Z}"/></span> bits of storage is compact, <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt8" id="mwIA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"Z + \\sqrt{Z}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle Z+{\sqrt {Z}}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>Z</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <msqrt>
            <mi>Z</mi>
          </msqrt>
        </mrow>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle Z+{\sqrt {Z}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/2fb7c9664603844f3560f4e6187555131393a32a" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:8.137ex; height:3.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle Z+{\sqrt {Z}}}"/></span> bits is succinct, <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt9" id="mwIQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"Z + \\lg Z"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle Z+\lg Z}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>Z</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>lg</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>Z</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle Z+\lg Z}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ba5e808be28c8b9fe5d40a76057242b2a9118127" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:8.398ex; height:2.509ex;" alt="{\displaystyle Z+\lg Z}"/></span> bits is also succinct, and <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt10" id="mwIg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"Z + 3"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle Z+3}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>Z</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mn>3</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle Z+3}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/9c7bf06278ef76ed8db82f7c8fe4201d8ecb6f1d" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.505ex; width:5.683ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle Z+3}"/></span> bits is implicit.</p>

<p id="mwIw">Implicit structures are thus usually reduced to storing information using some <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Permutation" title="Permutation" id="mwJA">permutation</a> of the input data; the most well-known example of this is the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heap_(data_structure)" title="Heap (data structure)" id="mwJQ">heap</a>.</p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwJg" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwJw" aria-labelledby="Succinct_indexable_dictionaries"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Succinct_indexable_dictionaries">Succinct indexable dictionaries</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Succinct_data_structure&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Succinct indexable dictionaries"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwKA">Succinct indexable dictionaries, also called <i id="mwKQ">rank/select</i> dictionaries, form the basis of a number of succinct representation techniques, including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Binary_trees" title="Binary trees" class="mw-redirect" id="mwKg">binary trees</a>, <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt11" id="mwKw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>k</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle k}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3c9a2c7b599b37105512c5d570edc034056dd40" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.211ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle k}"/></span>-ary trees and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multisets" title="Multisets" class="mw-redirect" id="mwLA">multisets</a>,<sup about="#mwt12" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-raman2002succinct_2-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"raman2002succinct"}}'><a href="#cite_note-raman2002succinct-2" id="mwLQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwLg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLw">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMA">]</span></span></a></sup> as well as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suffix_tree" title="Suffix tree" id="mwMQ">suffix trees</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suffix_array" title="Suffix array" id="mwMg">arrays</a>.<sup about="#mwt13" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-sadakane2006squeezing_3-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"sadakane2006squeezing"}}'><a href="#cite_note-sadakane2006squeezing-3" id="mwMw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwNA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNQ">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNg">]</span></span></a></sup> The basic problem is to store a subset <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt14" id="mwNw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>S</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/4611d85173cd3b508e67077d4a1252c9c05abca2" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.499ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S}"/></span> of a universe <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt15" id="mwOA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"U =\n[0 \\dots n) = \\{0, 1, \\dots, n - 1\\}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle U=[0\dots n)=\{0,1,\dots ,n-1\}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

<ul id="mwPA"><li id="mwPQ"><span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt19" id="mwPg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\mathbf{rank}_q(x) = |\\{ k \\in [0 \\dots x] : B[k] = q \\}|"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \mathbf {rank} _{q}(x)=|\{k\in [0\dots x]:B[k]=q\}|}">

    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle \mathbf {select} _{q}(x)=\min\{k\in [0\dots n):\mathbf {rank} _{q}(k)=x\}}</annotation>
  </semantics>

<p id="mwSg">There is a simple representation<sup about="#mwt28" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-jacobson1989space_4-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"jacobson1989space"}}'><a href="#cite_note-jacobson1989space-4" id="mwSw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwTA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTQ">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTg">]</span></span></a></sup> which uses <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt29" id="mwTw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n + o(n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n+o(n)}">

It uses an idea similar to that for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Range_Minimum_Query" title="Range Minimum Query" class="mw-redirect" id="mwUw">range-minimum queries</a>; there are a constant number of recursions before stopping at a subproblem of a limited size. The difference here is that it only needs <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt39" id="mwXg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\lg l = \\lg \\lg^2 n = 2 \\lg \\lg n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \lg l=\lg \lg ^{2}n=2\lg \lg n}">

<p id="mwbQ">In practice, the lookup table <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt50" id="mwbg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"R_p"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle R_{p}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>R</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>p</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle R_{p}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/f6650a6a5d41e666a0e83553ed13ec10e0d72845" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -1.005ex; width:2.823ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle R_{p}}"/></span> can be replaced by bitwise operations and smaller tables that can be used to find the number of bits set in the small blocks. This is often beneficial, since succinct data structures find their uses in large data sets, in which case cache misses become much more frequent and the chances of the lookup table being evicted from closer CPU caches becomes higher.<sup about="#mwt51" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-gonzález2005practical_5-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"gonzález2005practical"}}'><a href="#cite_note-gonzález2005practical-5" id="mwbw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwcA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcQ">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcg">]</span></span></a></sup> Select queries can be easily supported by doing a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Binary_search" title="Binary search" id="mwcw">binary search</a> on the same auxiliary structure used for <b id="mwdA">rank</b>; however, this takes <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt52" id="mwdQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(\\lg n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(\lg n)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>lg</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(\lg n)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/360147da5c0229b8b7fa2795ca33790c0d4b8003" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:7.174ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(\lg n)}"/></span> time in the worst case. A more complicated structure using <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt53" id="mwdg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"3n/\\lg \\lg n + O(\\sqrt{n} \\lg n \\lg \\lg n) = o(n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 3n/\lg \lg n+O({\sqrt {n}}\lg n\lg \lg n)=o(n)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mn>3</mn>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mo>/</mo>
        </mrow>
        <mi>lg</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>lg</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <msqrt>
            <mi>n</mi>
          </msqrt>
        </mrow>
        <mi>lg</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mi>lg</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>lg</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mi>o</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle 3n/\lg \lg n+O({\sqrt {n}}\lg n\lg \lg n)=o(n)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/6f7bcca9021daaf333ea3208aacc124c90eaddda" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -1.005ex; width:37.231ex; height:3.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 3n/\lg \lg n+O({\sqrt {n}}\lg n\lg \lg n)=o(n)}"/></span> bits of additional storage can be used to support <b id="mwdw">select</b> in constant time.<sup about="#mwt54" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-clark1998compact_6-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"clark1998compact"}}'><a href="#cite_note-clark1998compact-6" id="mweA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mweQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mweg">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwew">]</span></span></a></sup> In practice, many of these solutions have hidden constants in the <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt55" id="mwfA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(\\cdot)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(\cdot )}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mo>⋅<!-- ⋅ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle O(\cdot )}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c82fd5fbde213ba5a358a9b5f47775bc7e716137" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.229ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle O(\cdot )}"/></span> notation which dominate before any asymptotic advantage becomes apparent; implementations using broadword operations and word-aligned blocks often perform better in practice.<sup about="#mwt56" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-vigna2008broadword_7-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"vigna2008broadword"}}'><a href="#cite_note-vigna2008broadword-7" id="mwfQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwfg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfw">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwgA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwng">If one wishes to support insertions and deletions, it is possible to achieve a space bound of <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt72" id="mwnw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\mathcal{B}(m,n) + O(n / 2^{(\\log n)^{\\Omega(1)}})"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\mathcal {B}}(m,n)+O(n/2^{(\log n)^{\Omega (1)}})}">

<p id="mwpQ">It is also possible to construct a indexible dictionary supporting rank (but not select) that uses fewer than <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt77" id="mwpg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\textstyle \\mathcal{B}(m,n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \textstyle {\mathcal {B}}(m,n)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">

          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
              <mi class="MJX-tex-caligraphic" mathvariant="script">B</mi>
            </mrow>
          </mrow>
          <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
          <mi>m</mi>

          <mi>n</mi>

Such a dictionary is called a <i id="mwpw">monotone minimal perfect hash function</i>, and can be implemented using as few as <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt78" id="mwqA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"O(m \\log \\log \\log n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle O(m\log \log \log n)}">

  </semantics>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwsQ" aria-labelledby="Succinct_hash_tables"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Succinct_hash_tables">Succinct hash tables</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Succinct_data_structure&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Succinct hash tables"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwsg">A succinct <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hash_table" title="Hash table" id="mwsw">hash table</a>, also known as a <i id="mwtA">succinct unordered dictionary,</i> is a data structure that stores <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt85" id="mwtQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"m"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle m}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>m</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle m}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/0a07d98bb302f3856cbabc47b2b9016692e3f7bc" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:2.04ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle m}"/></span> keys from a universe <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt86" id="mwtg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\{0, 1, \\dots, n - 1\\}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \{0,1,\dots ,n-1\}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mo fence="false" stretchy="false">{</mo>
        <mn>0</mn>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mo>…<!-- … --></mo>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo fence="false" stretchy="false">}</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle \{0,1,\dots ,n-1\}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/83abd2a9b860d2cb7cbb2357e3308468fb8dcd4b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:16.26ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle \{0,1,\dots ,n-1\}}"/></span> using space <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt87" id="mwtw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"(1 + o(1)) \\mathcal{B}(m, n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle (1+o(1)){\mathcal {B}}(m,n)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>o</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>

        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi class="MJX-tex-caligraphic" mathvariant="script">B</mi>
          </mrow>
        </mrow>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle (1+o(1)){\mathcal {B}}(m,n)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/d63443756d4eddd21024f62bad601404d2d543ad" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:17.733ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle (1+o(1)){\mathcal {B}}(m,n)}"/></span> bits, and while supporting membership queries in constant expected time. If a succinct hash table also supports insertions and deletions in constant expected time, then it is referred to as <i id="mwuA">dynamic</i>, and otherwise it is referred to as <i id="mwuQ">static.</i></p>

<p id="mwug">The first dynamic succinct hash table was due to Raman and Rao in 2003.<sup about="#mwt90" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-13" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-13" id="mwuw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwvA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwvQ">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwvg">]</span></span></a></sup> In the case where <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt91" id="mwvw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n = \\text{poly}(m)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n={\text{poly}}(m)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mtext>poly</mtext>
        </mrow>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n={\text{poly}}(m)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/81ab94afbdecec8373a47be60f14830429178336" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:12.672ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n={\text{poly}}(m)}"/></span>, their solution uses space <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt92" id="mwwA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\mathcal{B}(m, n) + O(m \\log \\log m)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\mathcal {B}}(m,n)+O(m\log \log m)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

          </mrow>
        </mrow>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mo>,</mo>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>O</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mi>log</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>log</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\mathcal {B}}(m,n)+O(m\log \log m)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/fb191c9e748905791bf719e01cdd7dc8f9992882" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:25.43ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\mathcal {B}}(m,n)+O(m\log \log m)}"/></span> bits. Subsequently, it was shown that this space bound could be improved to <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt93" id="mwwQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\mathcal{B}(m, n) + O(m \\log \\log \\log \\cdots \\log m)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\mathcal {B}}(m,n)+O(m\log \log \log \cdots \log m)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/9e11ee0f4d09c4dedfaa88236887c2f781d2353e" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:35.258ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\mathcal {B}}(m,n)+O(m\log \log \log \cdots \log m)}"/></span> bits for any constant number of logarithms<sup about="#mwt96" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-14" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-14" id="mwwg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwww"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxA">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxQ">]</span></span></a></sup> and a little after that this bound was also optimal.<sup about="#mwt99" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-15" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-15" id="mwxg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwxw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyA">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt102" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-16" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-16" id="mwyg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwyw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwzA">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwzQ">]</span></span></a></sup> The latter solution supports all operations in worst-case constant time <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/With_high_probability" title="With high probability" id="mwzg">with high probability</a>.</p>

<p id="mwzw">The first static succinct hash table was due to Pagh in 1999.<sup about="#mwt105" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mw0A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw0Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0g">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0w">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt108" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_18-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:0-18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-18" id="mw1A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw1Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1g">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1w">]</span></span></a></sup> In the case where <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt109" id="mw2A" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n = \\text{poly}(m)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n={\text{poly}}(m)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mtext>poly</mtext>
        </mrow>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n={\text{poly}}(m)}</annotation>
  </semantics>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mw-w" aria-labelledby="Other_examples"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Other_examples">Other examples</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Succinct_data_structure&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Other examples"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mw_A">A string with an arbitrary length (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pascal_string" title="Pascal string" class="mw-redirect" id="mw_Q">Pascal string</a>) takes <i id="mw_g">Z</i> + log(<i id="mw_w">Z</i>) space, and is thus succinct. If there is a maximum length – which is the case in practice, since 2<sup id="mwAQA">32</sup> = 4 GiB of data is a very long string, and 2<sup id="mwAQE">64</sup> = 16 EiB of data is larger than any string in practice – then a string with a length is also implicit, taking <i id="mwAQI">Z</i> + <i id="mwAQM">k</i> space, where <i id="mwAQQ">k</i> is the number of data to represent the maximum length (e.g., 64 bits).</p>

<p id="mwAQU">When a sequence of variable-length items (such as strings) needs to be encoded, there are various possibilities. A direct approach is to store a length and an item in each record – these can then be placed one after another. This allows efficient next, but not finding the <i id="mwAQY">k</i>th item. An alternative is to place the items in order with a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delimiter" title="Delimiter" id="mwAQc">delimiter</a> (e.g., <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Null-terminated_string" title="Null-terminated string" id="mwAQg">null-terminated string</a>). This uses a delimiter instead of a length, and is substantially slower, since the entire sequence must be scanned for delimiters. Both of these are space-efficient. An alternative approach is out-of-band separation: the items can simply be placed one after another, with no delimiters. Item bounds can then be stored as a sequence of length, or better, offsets into this sequence. Alternatively, a separate binary string consisting of 1s in the positions where an item begins, and 0s everywhere else is encoded along with it. Given this string, the <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt128" id="mwAQk" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"select"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle select}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>s</mi>
        <mi>e</mi>
        <mi>l</mi>
        <mi>e</mi>
        <mi>c</mi>
        <mi>t</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle select}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c3f3575662915b7416952a259db96ea2c16a5d5c" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:5.797ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle select}"/></span> function can quickly determine where each item begins, given its index.<sup about="#mwt131" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-21" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-21"}}'><a href="#cite_note-21" id="mwAQo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQw">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQ0">]</span></span></a></sup> This is <i id="mwAQ4">compact</i> but not <i id="mwAQ8">succinct,</i> as it takes 2<i id="mwARA">Z</i> space, which is O(<i id="mwARE">Z</i>).</p>

<p id="mwARI">Another example is the representation of a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Binary_tree" title="Binary tree" id="mwARM">binary tree</a>: an arbitrary binary tree on <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt132" id="mwARQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a601995d55609f2d9f5e233e36fbe9ea26011b3b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.395ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n}"/></span> nodes can be represented in <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt133" id="mwARU" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"2n + o(n)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 2n+o(n)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mn>2</mn>
        <mi>n</mi>
        <mo>+</mo>
        <mi>o</mi>

  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/bb0b9882cca56b0c10216f0ca56dbb97e827b17f" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:9.729ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 2n+o(n)}"/></span> bits while supporting a variety of operations on any node, which includes finding its parent, its left and right child, and returning the size of its subtree, each in constant time. The number of different binary trees on <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt134" id="mwARY" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">

  <semantics>

For large <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt137" id="mwARk" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle n}">

    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a601995d55609f2d9f5e233e36fbe9ea26011b3b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.395ex; height:1.676ex;" alt="{\displaystyle n}"/></span>, this is about <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt138" id="mwARo" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"4^n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 4^{n}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msup>
          <mn>4</mn>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>n</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msup>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle 4^{n}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/bcdd6057d8f9857a7d45670ced53881c2e312f34" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:2.381ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 4^{n}}"/></span>; thus we need at least about <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt139" id="mwARs" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\log_2(4^n)=2n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \log _{2}(4^{n})=2n}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>log</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <msup>
          <mn>4</mn>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>n</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msup>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mn>2</mn>
        <mi>n</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle \log _{2}(4^{n})=2n}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/4a5e6f32331d62cc51537dc9a85be610ce60d44f" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:13.872ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle \log _{2}(4^{n})=2n}"/></span> bits to encode it. A succinct binary tree therefore would occupy only <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt140" id="mwARw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"2"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-inline mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle 2}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mn>2</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle 2}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/901fc910c19990d0dbaaefe4726ceb1a4e217a0f" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:1.162ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle 2}"/></span> bits per node.</p>
